Binary local search algorithm details:
Inputs: h binary, corr_self, n, time_limit, prefix maybe, rng, temp.
Maintain best_h, best_c, best_corr? We can keep current and best.
Trial generation:
- Option 1: random 2-swap: pick zero and one indices uniformly.
- Option 2: swap with neighbors (distances 1..D) to refine. We can precompute for each zero a list of candidate ones? Random enough.
- Option 3: block swap: choose a block of d bits and flip preserving sum? e.g., flip 0s to1 and 1s to0 within pairs.
Steepest:
 For a fixed number of trials, evaluate random swaps. For each, compute new_c. Update if better or accept anneal.
 Annealing: `temp = start_temp`, decay to end. Accept if `retry < log rng`? Need no lambdas. Use `new_c - cur_c < temp * log(rng)`. Since c values .3. start .005, end .0002.
 Also always track best non-increasing.
 To move current, if accept, commit flips; else revert.
But commit/revert flip functions with binary exact. Need maintain sum. For 2-swap, sum preserved.
We need handle if h is not strict binary (some fractions). Binary local search should start binary. `ensure_binary_feasible`.

Potential problem: 2-swap random-only may be slow to escape. Add random restarts: after some time, reset current to best or new start.

Local search pseudo:
```
def binary_local_search(h, n, budget_s, rng, temp_start=0.01, temp_end=0.0005, max_trial_time=0.1):
    deadline = time.time()+budget_s
    comp = 1.0 - h
    corr = compute_corr_self(h)
    cur_c = c5_from_corr(corr,n)
    best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy(); best_corr=corr.copy()
    trial_count=0
    temporal= temp_start
    while time.time()<deadline-1:
       # adjust temp based on fraction elapsed
       z=... ; temporal=...
       z_idx = zeros; o_idx=ones; use rng.choice
       old_z=h[z_idx]; old_o=h[o_idx]
       flip(z_idx,1); flip(o_idx,0)
       new_c = corr.max()*dx
       accept = new_c < cur_c - 1e-12
       if not accept and new_c < cur_c + temporal:
          # probabilistic
       if accept:
          cur_h = h; cur_corr=corr; cur_c=new_c
          if new_c < best_c:
             best=copy; best_c=new_c
       else:
          flip(o_idx,1); flip(z_idx,0)
          # use old comp/h
       trial_count +=1
       if trial_count%5000==0: recompute corr from h to clean drift.
    return best_h,best_c,best_corr
```
But copying h/corr for best only on improvement. We mutate h in place; if reject revert, h restored. Good.

Implement `set_value(h, comp, corr, j, new_val)` exact:
```
old = h[j]
delta = new_val - old
if abs(delta)<1e-15: return
# linear updates using current h and comp (1-h) before updating j
# first factor contribution for all i: p1 segment j..j+n-1 with weights comp[n-1-j? reversed]
corr[j:j+n] += delta * comp[j+n-1:j-1:-1]
# second factor: corr[n-1-j:2n-1-j] -= delta * h
# k=0 correction:
actual0 = new_val*(1-new_val) - old*(1-old)
linear0 = delta*(1.0 - 2.0*old)  # comes from first and second at p=n-1
corr[n-1] += actual0 - linear0
h[j]=new_val
comp[j]=1-new_val
```
Check for arbitrary: linear formula first uses comp[j]=1-old, second uses h[j]=old; correction adjusts p=n-1. Good.
This is exact for one coordinate (ignoring interactions with previously updated variables? It uses current comp/h for others, exact). We can use for pair moves.

Then moves preserving sum:
1. Swap values (i,j): set_value(i, h[j]); set_value(j, h[i]_old). Need store old_i,old_j.
2. Pair transfer: choose base values a,b; add δ to i (a+δ) and -δ to j (b-δ) clipped. This preserves sum. Evaluate objective; if better accept, else revert by set_value back old. δ can be small or to bounds.
3. Pair optimize: Given h_i=a,h_j=b, total s. Choose t ∈ [max(0,s-1),min(1,s)] for h_i=new, h_j=s-t. We can compute F(t) exactly? Incrementally applying set_values for each trial costly. But we can 1D sample t for selected pairs. This is exact two-coordinate optimization preserving sum! That's promising.

For a pair (i,j), with current a,b. Let t = new_i, u=s-t new_j. We can compute objective F(t) from scratch? Or use formulas with current corr and old values, update linear/quadratic terms affected by i and j. Since changing two values, each C_p is at most quadratic in t (due to k=0 for each and bilinear cross if pair at a lag). We can derive coefficients and minimize exactly/robustly. This would be a continuous local search over pairs preserving sum. Much stronger and sum exact.

Let's derive pair exact coefficients for self C_p. We have current h, corr. Change x_i from a to t, x_j from b to u=s-t. For each lag p, C_p = constant + linear terms + quadratic terms. Affected only p involving i or j. Since only two variables changed, C_p as a function of t is polynomial degree ≤2 (if both appear in same product? product h_i(1-h_j) if i and j at lag gives (t)(1-u)=t(1-s+t)=t - st + t^2, quadratic with +t^2. k=0 terms t(1-t) = -t^2, u(1-u) = quadratic. Sum may have t^2 coefficient. Also pair appears in two lags? Actually for each p, variables may appear linearly or quadratically.
We can compute for candidate pair by evaluating C_p(t) using direct formula for affected p? But we can compute coefficients A_p,B_p,C_p (quadratic) by considering contributions.
Simpler: For a selected pair, we can sample t values (e.g., endpoints and interior) and update twice per sample, compute max, choose best. But updating twice and max O(n) per sample. Pair count maybe thousands, samples 10 -> okay. We can use 1D minimization by sampling 16 points and local refine. This would be exact (up to grid) and preserve sum. Let's explore.

Function `try_pair_opt(h, comp, corr, i, j, dx, samples)`
- old_i, old_j, s=old_i+old_j, tlo=max(0,s-1), thi=min(1,s). If tlo==thi no.
- Determine current c. For t in grid [tlo...thi] plus maybe 0.5, compute C after applying pair changes. To avoid corruption, apply set_value i=t, j=s-t, compute max, then revert if not best or keep.     set_value(h, comp, corr, j, u)

For pair (i,j), with current h, corr. We can compute for each lag p the change as function of t (h_i=t, h_j=s-t). The final C_p = corr[p] + Δ_p(t). Full pair pass algorithm with coefficients:
```
def pair_case(deltas, corr, alpha, beta, dx):
   # corr current
   for d in grid:
       vals = corr + alpha*d + beta*d*d
       c = vals.max()*dx
```

`compute_corr_self` direct:
```
def compute_corr_self(h):
 n=len(h); M=2*n-1; C=np.zeros(M); v=1-h; C[n-1]=np.dot(h,v)
 for l in range(1,n):
    # k=l: h[l:] dot v[:-l]
    C[n-1+l] = np.dot(h[l:], v[:-l])
    # k=-l: h[:-l] dot v[l:]
    C[n-1-l] = np.dot(h[:-l], v[l:])
 return C
```
For n=2 l=1: C[2]=h[1]*v[0]=b(1-a); C[0]=h[0]*v[1]=a(1-b). matches.
Good.

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

G_N=0; G_SCALE=1e-3; G_TARGET=0.0; G_PEN=0.0

def compute_c5(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if len(h)==0: return np.inf
    dx=2.0/len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full'))*dx)

def compute_corr_self(h): ...

def c5_corr(corr,n): return float(np.max(corr)*2.0/n)

make_feasible details: To avoid breaking [0,1], robust:
```
def make_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
       s=np.sum(h)
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff) <= 1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          # add to entries with room, proportional to room to keep balanced
          room=1.0-h
          mask=room>1e-14
          if not mask.any(): break
          # distribute proportional to room: h[mask] += diff * room[mask]/room[mask].sum()
          total_room=room[mask].sum()
          inc = diff * room[mask] / total_room
          # but if diff <= total_room
          h[mask] += inc
       else:
          mass=h  # room to decrease
          mask=mass>1e-14
          if not mask.any(): break
          total_mass=mass[mask].sum()
          dec = diff * mass[mask] / total_mass # negative
          h[mask] += dec
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final adjust one coordinate if possible
    s=np.sum(h); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       # choose index with value near 0.5 to minimize clipping
       idx=int(np.argmin(np.abs(h-0.5)))
       val=h[idx]+diff
       if 0<=val<=1: h[idx]=val
       else:
          if diff>0:
             mask=h<1-1e-14
             if mask.any():
                cnt=mask.sum(); h[mask]+=diff/cnt
          else:
             mask=h>1e-14
             if mask.any():
                cnt=mask.sum(); h[mask]+=diff/cnt
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
This proportional distribution might change objective more but feasible.

def to_binary_feasible(h,n):
   b=np.zeros(n); idx=np.argpartition(h, n//2); b[np.argpartition?]=1
```

`smooth_fun` and globals:
```
G_N=0; G_SCALE=1e-3; G_TARGET=0.0
G_BOUNDS=None

def smooth_corr(h): maybe not needed.

def smooth_obj_value(x):
   n=G_N; corr=compute_corr_self(x); mx=corr.max(); if not finite...
   t=(corr-mx)/G_SCALE; e=np.exp(t); s=e.sum(); return mx+G_SCALE*np.log(s)

def smooth_obj_grad(x):
   n=G_N; corr=compute_corr_self(x); mx=corr.max(); t=...; e=...; w=e/s
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   # active weights >1e-8
   idxs=np.where(w>1e-9)[0]
   for p in idxs:
       k=p-(n-1); wp=w[p]
       if k>=0:
          # first

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start=time.time()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, budget_s) - 5.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # load parent
    n_parent=0; h_parent=None
    try:
      hp=np.array(initial_h_values,float)
      if hp.size>0:
         n_parent=len(hp); h_parent=make_feasible(hp,n_parent)
    except Exception: pass
    if h_parent is None:
       n_parent=160; h_parent=make_feasible(rng.rand(n_parent), n_parent)
    best_h=h_parent.copy(); best_n=n_parent; best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    # Quick pair descent on parent for maybe 300s
    h2,c2 = pair_local_search(best_h,best_n, deadline_alloc= min(300, deadline-time-...), rng=rng)
    if c2 < best_c: update
    # SLSQP at parent if time
    h3,c3 = slsqp_polish(... deadline start+60?) update
    # binary starts at parent and maybe 2n
    for n in [best_n, maybe 2*best_n, 4*best_n, 256, 320, 512] unique sorted <=512:
        if remaining < 60: break
        # generate starts
        starts=[]
        # parent interpolated to n, binary and continuous
        h_cont=interpolate_to_n(best_h,n) if n!=best_n else best_h
        starts.append(h_cont)
        starts.append(to_binary_feasible(h_cont,n))
        # a few random scored binary
        for i in range(3): starts.append(scored_binary(n, rng, variant=i))
        for h0 in starts:
            if remaining < 40: break
            # choose time per start based n
            if n <=200: pair_time = min(120, remaining-40)
            elif n <=400: pair_time= min(60, remaining-40)
            else: pair_time=min(40, remaining-40)
            h_opt,c_opt = pair_local_search(h0,n,deadline=time.time()+pair_time, rng=rng, random_pairs=True if n>256 else False)
            if c_opt < best_c: update
            # if n<=220, SLSQP final 30s
    # final global pair on best_n for remaining
    h_final,c_final=pair_local_search(best_h,best_n, deadline=deadline, rng=rng)
    if c_final<best_c: update
    # final SLSQP if time and n<=256
    # final make_feasible, compute c5_np
    return (best_h,best_np? actually best_n, len)

Let's define `random_scored_binary(n, rng, variant)`:
```
def scored_binary(n,rng,variant):
    x=np.arange(n)/n
    if variant==0:
       scores = np.sin(2*np.pi*x) + 0.5*np.sin(4*np.pi*x + rng.rand())
    elif variant==1:
       scores = np.sin(2*np.pi*2*x + rng.rand()) + 0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*5*x)
    elif variant==2:
       # hash: (i*7919 % 9973)/9973
       scores = ((np.arange(n)*7919) % 9973) / 9973.0
    else:
       scores = rng.rand(n)
    scores += 1e-6*rng.rand(n)
    b=np.zeros(n)
    idx=np.argpartition(scores, n//2)[:n//2] # if n//2 exact; argpartition kth n//2 maybe if k==n? For n even k=n/2 <n. good.
    b[idx]=1
    return b
```
If n=0 no. For exact count n//2 (if n even). For odd? choose floor, but target sum n/2 not integer if odd; binary can't exactly half? For odd, cannot have exactly n/2 integer with binary values. We should only use even n. Parent likely 160 even. For selected n, make even: if n%2, n+=1. In run choose even.

`to_binary_feasible(h,n)`:
```
def to_binary_feasible(h,n):
    target=n//2
    order=np.argpartition(h, target)
    vals=np.zeros(n)
    smallest=order[:target]
    # If duplicates at boundary and target? top arbitrary; fine.
    vals[smallest]=1
    return vals
```
But if target=0? no. Use `np.argsort(-h)[:target]` if n small; argpartition faster. For exact count, good. But parent may have exactly target high values; no issue.

`compute_corr_self(h)`:
```
def compute_corr_self(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=float)
    M = 2*n - 1
    out = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    v = 1.0 - h
    out[n-1] = np.dot(h, v)
    for lag in range(1, n):
        # positive lag
        out[n-1 + lag] = np.dot(h[lag:], v[:-lag])
        # negative lag
        out[n-1 - lag] = np.dot(h[:-lag], v[lag:])
    return out
```
For large n, Python loop n dot. Could optimize using FFT? For n=512, 511 dots okay. For many recomputes maybe. Use `np.correlate`? But need self order; maybe `np.correlate` output matches self and implemented C direct? For recomputing, `np.correlate(h,v,'full')` may be faster than Python dots. But if it matches self, we can use it. To be safe and speed, use `np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')`. But in pair coefficient derivation, we assume self order. If np.correlate matches, fine. We can test mentally yes. Use np.correlate for recomputes to speed. For initial corr in pair search, use `compute_corr_self`? We can define `correlate_self = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')`. That should match and is C. But if formula sign reversed, max same but updates alignment? We need order for p indices; np.correlate likely same as convolution with reversed v as we derived. I think it's same. To be absolutely safe, we could implement a one-time check in run: compare my compute_corr_self on a random small array? But not needed. Maybe use my direct for alignment and speed? Direct Python loop for n=160 maybe okay; for n=512, 511 dots with average length256 =>131k multiply, C plus 511 Python calls; maybe 0.001s. Numpy correlate direct C maybe 0.0001? Use np.correlate to reduce Python. But if order differs, coefficient update may be wrong. Need know exactly.

Let's confirm NumPy correlate convention. `np.correlate(a,v)` returns `c_k = sum_n a[n+k] * v[n]` for k=-(Nv-1)..(Na-1)? For full output index? For a length Na, v length Nv. If c_k = sum_n a[n+k] conj(v[n]), with k from -(Nv-1) to Na-1. Output index k+(Nv-1). For a=h, v=1-h, Nv=n. Output p= k+n-1. c_k = sum_n h[n+k](1-h[n]). This is exactly self C_k! For k positive; for k negative, h[n+k] where n>=-k; sum h[n+k](1-h[n]) = self C_k? We defined self for k negative as ∑_{i=k}^{n-1} h[i+k](1-h[i]) with i=n? For k=-l, sum_{n=l}^{n-1} h[n-l](1-h[n]) = ∑ r=0^{n-1-l} h[r](1-h[r+l]), matches. So yes np.correlate matches self. Great. Use np.correlate for speed.

`scored_binary`:
```
def scored_binary(n, rng, variant):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    u = x / float(n)
    if variant == 0:
        ph=rng.rand()*2*np.pi
        scores=np.sin(2*np.pi*u+ph)+0.4*np.sin(4*np.pi*u+ph*1.3)
    elif variant ==1:
        ph=rng.rand()*2*np.pi
        scores=np.sin(2*np.pi*3*u+ph)+0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*7*u)+0.2*rng.rand(n)
    elif variant==2:
        # GCD low discrepancy
        scores=((x*7919)%10007).astype(float)/10007.0
        scores += 1e-6*rng.rand(n)
    else:
        scores=rng.rand(n)
    target=n//2
    if target <=0 or target>=n:
       return make_feasible(s*..., n)
    idx=np.argpartition(scores, target)[:target]
    b=np.zeros(n); b[idx]=1.0
    return b
```

Define:
```
def pair_evaluate_best(corr, h, comp, i, j, n, dx, grid, alpha, local_grid):
    a = h[i]; b = h[j]; s = a+b
    lo = max(0.0, s-1.0); hi = min(1.0, s)
    if hi - lo <= 1e-13: return 0.0, None, 0.0
    dlo = lo - a; dhi = hi - a
    span = dhi - dlo
    if span <= 1e-13: return 0.0, None, 0.0
    M=2*n-1
    alpha.fill(0.0)
    comp_rev = comp[n-1::-1]
    alpha[i:i+n] += comp_rev
    alpha[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h
    alpha[j:j+n] -= comp_rev
    alpha[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h
    p0 = n-1
    p1 = p0 + (j - i)
    p2 = p0 + (i - j)
    deltas = dlo + span * grid
    # Maybe skip d=0? We can evaluate all, best could be d=0 with c current. But current_c outside; if best d=0 no move.
    vals = corr[None, :] + alpha[None, :] * deltas[:, None]
    d2 = deltas * deltas
    vals[:, p0] -= 2.0 * d2
    vals[:, p1] += d2
    vals[:, p2] += d2
    cvals = vals.max(axis=1) * dx
    best_rate = int(np.argmin(cvals))
    best_c = float(cvals[best_rate])
    best_d = float(deltas[best_rate])
    # local refine if allowed and internal
    if local_grid is not None:
       best_frac = best_rate / (len(grid)-1)
       step = 1.0 / (len(grid)-1)
       lf = best_frac + step * local_grid  # offsets relative maybe [-1,-0.5,0,0.5,1]
       lf = lf[(lf >= -1e-12) & (lf <= 1+1e-12)]
       lf = np.clip(lf,0.0,1.0)
       if lf.size > 0:
          deltas_l = dlo + span * lf
          vals_l = corr[None,:] + alpha[None,:]*deltas_l[:,None]
          d2l = deltas_l*deltas_l
          vals_l[:,p0] -= 2.0*d2l
          vals_l[:,p1] += d2l
          vals_l[:,p2] += d2l
          cvals_l = vals_l.max(axis=1)*dx
          bl = int(np.argmin(cvals_l))
          if cvals_l[bl] < best_c - 1e-15:
              best_c = float(cvals_l[bl]); best_d=float(deltas_l[bl])
    return best_d, best_c
```
Return best_d, best_c (or best_c None if no range). The caller uses if best_c < cur_c - tol. It also needs alpha (filled). If no range, alpha maybe zero. If p1/p2 maybe. If grid length 1? Use at least 3.

Function `pair_build_alpha`:
```
def pair_build_alpha(h, comp, alpha, i, j, n):
    alpha.fill(0.0)
    comp_rev = comp[n-1::-1]
    alpha[i:i+n] += comp_rev
    alpha[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h
    alpha[j:j+n] -= comp_rev
    alpha[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h
```
`comp_rev` view. `alpha[i:i+n]` in-place add with broadcast comp_rev length n. Good.

Let's write `pair_local_search` with these helpers.
```
def pair_local_search(h,n,budget_s,rng,full_pairs=True):
    h = make_feasible(h,n).copy()
    M=2*n-1
    comp = 1.0 - h
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    dx=2.0/n
    cur_c = float(np.max(corr)*dx)
    best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy(); best_corr=corr.copy()
    pts=pair_grid_points(n)
    grid=get_grid(pts)
    local_grid = np.array([-1.0,-0.5,0.0,0.5,1.0]) if pts>=15 else None
    max_rows = pts if local_grid is None else max(pts,5)
    work=np.empty((max_rows,M),float)
    cbuf=np.empty(max_rows,float)
    deltas_buf=np.empty(max_rows,float)
    local_fracs_buf=np.empty(5,float)
    alpha=np.zeros(M,float)
    deadline=time.time()+max(1.0,budget_s)
    accept=0; since_accept=0
    if full_pairs:
       while time.time() < deadline - 0.2:
          order = np.arange(n)
          if rng is not None and n <= 300:
             order = rng.permutation(n)
          pass_acc=0
          for ii in order:
             i=int(ii)
             if time.time() > deadline-0.2: break
             for j in range(i+1,n):
                bc,bd = pair_evaluate_best(...)
                if bc < cur_c - 1e-13:
                   ... commit
                   pass_acc+=1; accept+=1; since_accept=0
                   if cur_c < best_c - 1e-13: best...
                else: since_accept +=1
             # optional if time
          if pass_acc==0:
             break
          # after pass, drift check
          if accept % 512 ==0 or True: maybe recompute if n<=300? Could be expensive. At each pass recompute to sync; n=160 cost small.
          corr=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); comp=1-h; cur_c=...
          if cur_c > best_c + 1e-10:
             h=best_h.copy(); comp=1-h; corr=best_corr.copy(); cur_c=best_c
          else:
             # current is feasible, comp updated
             # if current state not best but larger, continue; if cur_c < best_c? impossible if best saved? Actually cur_c may be >best_c. We could restore best to avoid continuing from worse. If cur_c > best_c + 1e-12: restore best. Use strict.
    else:
       while time...:
          i = int(rng.randint(n)); j = int(rng.randint(n)); if i==j continue; if i>j swap
          bc,bd=pair_evaluate_best
          if bc < cur_c - 1e-13: commit; since_accept=0; if best...
          else: since_accept +=1
          if since_accept > 20000:
             # reset to best
             h=best_h.copy(); comp=1-h; corr=best_corr.copy(); cur_c=best_c; since_accept=0
          if accept >0 and accept%2048==0: recompute/drift...
    # end
    bh=make_feasible(best_h,n)
    bc_np? pair search returns self c of best_h. Let's recompute self:
    corr_b = np.correlate(bh,1-bh,'full')
    bc = float(np.max(corr_b)*2/n)
    return bh,bc
```
In full pass, if cur_c > best_c, continue from current worse? It's okay but could waste; restore best if worse by >1e-12 after pass. def pair_evaluate_best(corr, h, comp, i, j, n, dx, grid, local_grid, local_fracs_buf, delta, work, cbuf, alpha, current_c):
    a=h[i]; b=h[j]; s=a+b
    lo = a+b-1.0; if lo<0 lo=0
    hi = a+b; if hi>1 hi=1
    span = hi-lo
    ...
    dlo=lo-a; dhi=hi-a; span = dhi-dlo
    pair_build_alpha(...)
    p0=n-1; p1=p0+j-i; p2=p0-i+j (i-j)
    m = pair_eval_grid(corr, alpha, dlo, span, grid, p0,p1,p2, dx, work, cbuf, delta)
    best_idx = int(np.argmin(cbuf[:m]))
    best_c = float(cbuf[best_idx]); best_d=float(delta[best_idx]); best_frac=best_idx/(m-1)
    if local_grid is not None and best_c < current_c - 1e-13 and best_frac > 1e-6 and best_frac < 1.0-1e-6:
       step = 1.0/(m-1)
       center = best_frac
       l = local_grid.size
       local_fracs_buf[:l] = center + step*local_grid
       local_fracs_buf[:l] = np.clip(local_fracs_buf[:l],0.0,1.0)
       # optionally unique not needed
       m2 = pair_eval_grid(corr, alpha, dlo, span, local_fracs_buf[:l], ...)
       idx2=int(np.argmin(cbuf[:m2]))
       if cbuf[idx2] < best_c - 1e-15:
          best_c=float(cbuf[idx2]); best_d=float(delta[idx2])
    return best_c,best_d
```

In `smooth_obj_value`, if n=0 return 0.
```
def smooth_objective(x):
    n = G_N
    if n <=0: return 0.0
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full')
    vals = corr * (2.0 / n)
    mx = vals.max()
    t = (vals - mx) / G_SCALE
    e = np.exp(t)
    return float(mx + G_SCALE * np.log(e.sum()))
```
If G_SCALE very small, t for inactive ~ -large, exp underflow to 0. ok. If all vals zero? no.
`smooth_jacobian(x)`:
```
n=G_N; corr=np.correlate(...); vals=corr*dx; mx=...; w=.../sum; grad=np.zeros(n)
active = np.where(w > 1e-10)[0]
for p in active:
    k = int(p - (n-1)); wp = w[p]
    if k >= 0:
       # j from k to n-1
       grad[k:n] += wp * (1.0 - x[:n-k]) * dx
       grad[:n-k] -= wp * x[k:n] * dx
    else:
       l=-k
       grad[:n-l] += wp * (1.0 - x[l:n]) * dx
       grad[l:n] -= wp * x[:n-l] * dx
return grad
```
Check slices: For k>=0, first derivative for j=k..n-1: 1-h[j-k] = 1-x[0:n-k]. grad[k:] +=. second for j=0..n-k-1: -x[j+k]= -x[k:]. Good.
For k<0 l: first for j=0..n-l-1: 1-x[j+l] = 1-x[l:n]. grad[:n-l] +=. second for j=l..n-1: -x[j-l] = -x[:n-l]. grad[l:] -=. Good. Multiply dx.
If active empty? weights sum 1, not empty.

But smooth objective uses `np.exp(t)`, t max 0, min can be -1000, underflow. For active threshold maybe. Sum of exp could underflow if scale tiny and many? max exp 1, sum≥1. ok.

SLSQP function:
```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,budget_s):
    start=time.time(); deadline=start+max(1,budget_s)-2
    h=make_feasible(h,n).copy()
    best_h=h; best_c=compute_c5(h)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'jac':sum_constraint_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    scales=[1e-2,5e-3,2e-3,1e-3,5e-4,2e-4]
    for scale in scales:
        if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
        G_N = n; G_SCALE = float(scale); G_TARGET = 0.5*n
        maxiter = 200
        try:
           res = minimize(smooth_objective, h, method='SLSQP', jac=smooth_jacobian, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12})
        except Exception:
           break
        if hasattr(res,'x'):
           hr=make_feasible(res.x,n); cr=compute_c5(hr)
           if cr < best_c - 1e-14:
              best_h=hr; best_c=cr
              h=hr.copy()
           else:
              # keep previous h for next scale? maybe best_h
              h=best_h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```
But `G_N` etc are global; need declare `global G_N, G_SCALE` in slsqp_polish to assign. Yes top-level function can use `global`.

Let's think about algorithm correctness of pair coefficient in terms of sum-preserving δ. For a pair with a+b=s, h_i=a+δ,h_j=b-δ. The change in corr for a lag that includes both variables cross adds β=+1 δ². If a pair appears both as cross and as separate for same lag? For a fixed lag, variable i can appear in at most two terms: if k=i-other (first) and if other=i+k (second). If j also equals those others? There could be two distinct products involving i and j in the same lag: one where i first j second, another where j first i second? For a fixed k, both would require j=i+k and i=j+k => k=0, impossible for i≠j. But within same lag, i can also pair with another r and j with another s. α sums all separate. β only if i and j pair together (one product). Good.

For k=0, p0: each variable self-term; β=-2. They do not cross. Good.

Therefore exact.

Let's verify `pair_build_alpha` for variable j with δ -1: `alpha[j:j+n] -= comp_rev`; `alpha[n-1-j:2n-1-j] += h`. For p where variable j is second in a term with first r=j+k: p = n-1+j-k? Wait second update segment originally: second factor variable j changing by delta: corr[n-1-j:2n-1-j] -= delta * h, with p = n-1-j+r (r first). For delta=-1, contribution = -(-1)*h = +h. Good. First factor variable j: p = n-1+j-q, q second; contribution delta*(1-h_q)= -1*comp_rev. For p segment j..j+n-1 ascending, comp_rev order as q n-1..0? For variable j first, p = n-1+j-q, q from n-1 down to0 => p from j to j+n-1? Check j=1,n=5: q=4 p=1; q=0 p=5. So p ascending j..j+n-1, q descending n-1..0. `alpha[j:j+n] -= comp_rev` where comp_rev [q=n-1..0]. Good.

Implementation:
```
def make_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(100):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            mask = room > 1e-14
            if not np.any(mask): break
            room_tot = float(np.sum(room[mask]))
            if room_tot <= 0: break
            step = min(diff, room_tot)
            h[mask] += step * (room[mask] / room_tot)
            h = np.clip(h,0,1)
        else:
            mass = h
            mask = mass > 1e-14
            if not np.any(mask): break
            mass_tot = float(np.sum(mass[mask]))
            if mass_tot <=0: break
            step = max(diff, -mass_tot)  # negative or more negative? diff<0, -mass_tot<0; want magnitude <= mass_tot: step = max(diff, -mass_tot) (e.g. diff=-1, mass=0.5 => max(-1,-0.5)=-0.5)
            h[mask] += step * (mass[mask] / mass_tot)
            h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final one adj
    s=float(np.sum(h)); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       idx=int(np.argmin(np.abs(h-0.5)))
       val = h[idx]+diff
       if val >= -1e-14 and val <= 1+1e-14:
           val = min(1.0,max(0.0,val))
           h[idx]=val
       else:
           if diff>0:
              mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-14
              if np.any(mask):
                  cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
                  h[mask] += diff/cnt
           else:
              mask = h > 1e-14
              if np.any(mask):
                  cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
                  h[mask] += diff/cnt
           h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
If h all 1 and diff>0 impossible. Good.

`interpolate_to_n`: if len src <2 return make_feasible half? But n even.
```
def interpolate_to_n(src,n):
    src=np.asarray(src,float)
    if n <=1: return make_feasible(np.ones(n)*0.5,n)
    if src.size == n: return make_feasible(src,n)
    xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,src.size,endpoint=False)
    xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    hi=np.interp(xn,xo,src)
    return make_feasible(hi,n)
```

```
def sum_constraint(x,n): return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*float(n))
def sum_constraint_jac(x,n): return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
```

def make_start_list(n, base_h, base_n, rng, max_starts):
    starts=[]
    if base_h is None:
       starts.append(scored_binary(n,rng,0)); return starts[:max_starts]
    if n == base_n:
       starts.append(base_h.copy())
    else:
       hi = interpolate_to_n(base_h,n)
       starts.append(hi)
       starts.append(to_binary_feasible(hi,n))
    # add binary of base if same? maybe if n==base_n
    if len(starts) < max_starts:
       starts.append(to_binary_feasible(base_h if n==base_n else interpolate_to_n(base_h,n), n)) # may duplicate
    variants=[0,1,2]
    for v in variants:
       if len(starts) >= max_starts: break
       starts.append(scored_binary(n,rng,v))
    return starts[:max_starts]
```

Now, test small n mentally:
n=2. pair_search full: i=0,j=1. pair_build_alpha:
alpha size3. comp_rev length2. alpha[0:2]+=comp_rev; alpha[1:3]-=h; alpha[1:3]-=comp_rev; alpha[0:2]+=h. alpha?
If h=[0,1], comp=[1,0], comp_rev=[0,1]. alpha[0:2]+[0,1]=[0,1]; alpha[1:3]-=[0,1] => alpha [0,0,1]; -=comp_rev ([0,1]) -> [0,-1,1]; +=h [0,1] -> [0,0,2]. s=1, d∈[0,1], p0=1,p1=2,p2=0. corr old: [0,0,1] (a=0,b=1: C0=0,C1=0,C2=1). For d=1: C=corr+alpha= [0,0,3]; adjust p0 -2 -> [-0? p0 index1: 0-2=-2? Wait corr+alpha d=1: [0,0,3]; p0=1: C1 becomes -2? That's impossible. Something wrong! Let's compute. h=[0,1], swap to [1,0]. New corr should C0=1? self: k=-1: h0? [1,0] -> C0 = h[0]*(1-h[1]) =1*1=1; C1=1*0+0*1=0; C2=h[1]*(1-h[0])=0. Expected [1,0,0]. Our corr+alpha gave [0,0,3], p0 adjust -2 -> C1=-2, p1 p2 adjust +1 -> C0? p2 index0 +1 =>1; p1 index2 +1 =>4. [1,-2,4], not expected. Our alpha calculation is wrong? Let's debug.
Using formula for pair move h_i=0+δ, h_j=1-δ. For n=2, i=0,j=1, s=1.
C0 (p0,n-1=1) self terms: old 0, new (δ(1-δ)+(1-δ)δ?) Wait h0=δ,h1=1-δ. C0=δ(1-δ)+(1-δ)δ=2δ-2δ². For δ=1 =>0. Old 0. Δ=2δ-2δ². α at p0 should 2, β -2.
C1? p1 index2 k=1: h1*(1-h0) = (1-δ)*(1-δ?) Wait h1=1-δ, 1-h0=1-δ -> (1-δ)^2. Old δ=0:1. Δ =1 -2δ + δ² -1 = -2δ+δ². α=-? β=+1. For δ=1:0.
C2? p2 index0 k=-1: h0*(1-h1)=δ*δ=δ². Old0. Δ=δ². α=0,β=+1.
So total Δ: p0 α=2,β=-2; p1 α=-2,β=+1; p2 α=0,β=+1.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

G_N = 0
G_SCALE = 1e-3
G_TARGET = 0.0

_GRID_CACHE = {}
_LOCAL_OFFSETS = np.array([-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0], dtype=float)


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def make_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    for _ in range(80):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            mask = room > 1e-14
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            room_tot = float(np.sum(room[mask]))
            if room_tot <= 0.0:
                break
            step = min(diff, room_tot)
            h[mask] += step * (room[mask] / room_tot)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            mass = h
            mask = mass > 1e-14
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            mass_tot = float(np.sum(mass[mask]))
            if mass_tot <= 0.0:
                break
            step = max(diff, -mass_tot)
            h[mask] += step * (mass[mask] / mass_tot)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    s = float(np.sum(h))
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        idx = int(np.argmin(np.abs(h - 0.5)))
        val = h[idx] + diff
        if -1e-14 <= val <= 1.0 + 1e-14:
            h[idx] = min(1.0, max(0.0, val))
        else:
            if diff > 0.0:
                mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-14
                if np.any(mask):
                    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
                    h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
            else:
                mask = h > 1e-14
                if np.any(mask):
                    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
                    h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def interpolate_to_n(src, n):
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=float)
    if n <= 1:
        return make_feasible(np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=float) * 0.5, max(1, n))
    if src.size == n:
        return make_feasible(src, n)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, src.size, endpoint=False)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    return make_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, src), n)


def to_binary_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        h = interpolate_to_n(h, n)
    target = n // 2
    if target <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argpartition(h, target)[:target]
    b[idx] = 1.0
    return b


def scored_binary(n, rng, variant):
    target = n // 2
    if target <= 0 or target >= n:
        return make_feasible(np.ones(n, dtype=float) * 0.5, n)

    u = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    xi = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)

    if variant == 0:
        ph = float(rng.rand()) * (2.0 * np.pi)
        scores = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * u + ph) + 0.45 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * u + 1.31 * ph)
    elif variant == 1:
        ph = float(rng.rand()) * (2.0 * np.pi)
        scores = np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * u + ph) + 0.35 * np.cos(14.0 * np.pi * u) + 0.08 * rng.rand(n)
    elif variant == 2:
        scores = ((xi * 7919) % 10007).astype(float) / 10007.0
        scores += 1e-6 * rng.rand(n)
    else:
        scores = rng.rand(n)

    scores += 1e-10 * rng.rand(n)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argpartition(scores, target)[:target]
    b[idx] = 1.0
    return b


def get_grid(pts):
    if pts not in _GRID_CACHE:
        _GRID_CACHE[pts] = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, pts, dtype=float)
    return _GRID_CACHE[pts]


def pair_grid_points(n):
    if n <= 200:
        return 31
    if n <= 400:
        return 21
    return 13


def pair_build_alpha(h, comp, alpha, i, j, n):
    alpha.fill(0.0)
    comp_rev = comp[n - 1::-1]

    alpha[i:i + n] += comp_rev
    alpha[n - 1 - i:2 * n - 1 - i] -= h

    alpha[j:j + n] -= comp_rev
    alpha[n - 1 - j:2 * n - 1 - j] += h


def pair_eval_grid(corr, alpha, dlo, span, fracs, p0, p1, p2, dx, work, cbuf, delta):
    m = int(fracs.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return 0

    delta[:m] = dlo + span * fracs
    np.multiply(alpha, delta[:m, None], out=work[:m])
    work[:m] += corr

    d2 = delta[:m] * delta[:m]
    work[:m, p0] -= 2.0 * d2
    work[:m, p1] += d2
    work[:m, p2] += d2

    cbuf[:m] = np.max(work[:m], axis=1) * dx
    return m


def pair_evaluate_best(corr, h, comp, i, j, n, dx, grid, local_grid,
                       local_fracs_buf, delta, work, cbuf, alpha, current_c):
    a = float(h[i])
    b = float(h[j])
    s = a + b

    lo = s - 1.0
    if lo < 0.0:
        lo = 0.0
    hi = s
    if hi > 1.0:
        hi = 1.0

    dlo = lo - a
    dhi = hi - a
    span = dhi - dlo
    if span <= 1e-14:
        return float("inf"), 0.0

    pair_build_alpha(h, comp, alpha, i, j, n)

    p0 = n - 1
    p1 = p0 + (j - i)
    p2 = p0 - (j - i)

    m = pair_eval_grid(corr, alpha, dlo, span, grid, p0, p1, p2, dx, work, cbuf, delta)
    if m <= 0:
        return float("inf"), 0.0

    best_idx = int(np.argmin(cbuf[:m]))
    best_c = float(cbuf[best_idx])
    best_d = float(delta[best_idx])
    best_frac = float(best_idx) / float(m - 1)

    if (local_grid is not None and
            best_c < current_c - 1e-13 and
            best_frac > 1e-6 and
            best_frac < 1.0 - 1e-6):
        step = 1.0 / float(m - 1)
        ell = int(local_grid.size)
        local_fracs_buf[:ell] = best_frac + step * local_grid
        local_fracs_buf[:ell] = np.clip(local_fracs_buf[:ell], 0.0, 1.0)
        m2 = pair_eval_grid(corr, alpha, dlo, span, local_fracs_buf[:ell],
                            p0, p1, p2, dx, work, cbuf, delta)
        if m2 > 0:
            idx2 = int(np.argmin(cbuf[:m2]))
            if float(cbuf[idx2]) < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = float(cbuf[idx2])
                best_d = float(delta[idx2])

    return best_c, best_d


def pair_local_search(h, n, budget_s, rng, full_pairs=True):
    start = time.time()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget_s)) - 1.0

    h = make_feasible(h, n).copy()
    n = int(h.size)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    comp = 1.0 - h
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    cur_c = float(np.max(corr) * dx)

    best_c = cur_c
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_corr = corr.copy()

    pts = pair_grid_points(n)
    grid = get_grid(pts)
    use_local = pts >= 15
    local_grid = _LOCAL_OFFSETS if use_local else None
    max_rows = max(pts, 5)

    work = np.empty((max_rows, M), dtype=float)
    cbuf = np.empty(max_rows, dtype=float)
    delta = np.empty(max_rows, dtype=float)
    local_fracs_buf = np.empty(5, dtype=float)
    alpha = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)

    accepts = 0
    since_accept = 0

    if full_pairs:
        while time.time() < deadline - 0.2:
            if rng is not None and n <= 300:
                order = rng.permutation(n)
            else:
                order = np.arange(n)

            pass_accepts = 0

            for ii in order:
                i = int(ii)
                if time.time() > deadline - 0.2:
                    break
                for j in range(i + 1, n):
                    a = float(h[i])
                    b = float(h[j])

                    bc, bd = pair_evaluate_best(
                        corr, h, comp, i, j, n, dx, grid, local_grid,
                        local_fracs_buf, delta, work, cbuf, alpha, cur_c
                    )

                    if bc < cur_c - 1e-13 and abs(bd) > 1e-10:
                        ni = a + bd
                        nj = b - bd

                        if ni < 0.0:
                            ni = 0.0
                        elif ni > 1.0:
                            ni = 1.0
                        if nj < 0.0:
                            nj = 0.0
                        elif nj > 1.0:
                            nj = 1.0

                        h[i] = ni
                        h[j] = nj
                        comp[i] = 1.0 - ni
                        comp[j] = 1.0 - nj

                        if abs(ni - (a + bd)) > 1e-12 or abs(nj - (b - bd)) > 1e-12:
                            corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                            cur_c = float(np.max(corr) * dx)
                        else:
                            np.multiply(alpha, bd, out=work[0, :])
                            corr += work[0, :]
                            bd2 = bd * bd
                            p0 = n - 1
                            p1 = p0 + (j - i)
                            p2 = p0 - (j - i)
                            corr[p0] += -2.0 * bd2
                            corr[p1] += bd2
                            corr[p2] += bd2
                            cur_c = bc

                        pass_accepts += 1
                        accepts += 1
                        since_accept = 0

                        if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14:
                            best_c = cur_c
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            best_corr = corr.copy()

            if pass_accepts == 0:
                break

            if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
                break

            comp = 1.0 - h
            corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
            cur_c = float(np.max(corr) * dx)

            if cur_c > best_c + 1e-10:
                h = best_h.copy()
                comp = 1.0 - h
                corr = best_corr.copy()
                cur_c = best_c

    else:
        while time.time() < deadline - 0.2:
            i = int(rng.randint(n))
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            if i > j:
                i, j = j, i

            a = float(h[i])
            b = float(h[j])

            bc, bd = pair_evaluate_best(
                corr, h, comp, i, j, n, dx, grid, local_grid,
                local_fracs_buf, delta, work, cbuf, alpha, cur_c
            )

            if bc < cur_c - 1e-13 and abs(bd) > 1e-10:
                ni = a + bd
                nj = b - bd

                if ni < 0.0:
                    ni = 0.0
                elif ni > 1.0:
                    ni = 1.0
                if nj < 0.0:
                    nj = 0.0
                elif nj > 1.0:
                    nj = 1.0

                h[i] = ni
                h[j] = nj
                comp[i] = 1.0 - ni
                comp[j] = 1.0 - nj

                if abs(ni - (a + bd)) > 1e-12 or abs(nj - (b - bd)) > 1e-12:
                    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                    cur_c = float(np.max(corr) * dx)
                else:
                    np.multiply(alpha, bd, out=work[0, :])
                    corr += work[0, :]
                    bd2 = bd * bd
                    p0 = n - 1
                    p1 = p0 + (j - i)
                    p2 = p0 - (j - i)
                    corr[p0] += -2.0 * bd2
                    corr[p1] += bd2
                    corr[p2] += bd2
                    cur_c = bc

                accepts += 1
                since_accept = 0

                if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = cur_c
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_corr = corr.copy()
            else:
                since_accept += 1

            if since_accept > 30000:
                break

            if accepts > 0 and accepts % 2048 == 0:
                comp = 1.0 - h
                corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                cur_c = float(np.max(corr) * dx)
                if cur_c > best_c + 1e-10:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    comp = 1.0 - h
                    corr = best_corr.copy()
                    cur_c = best_c
                    since_accept = 0

    bh = make_feasible(best_h, n)
    bh_corr = np.correlate(bh, 1.0 - bh, mode="full")
    return bh, float(np.max(bh_corr) * dx)


def sum_constraint(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))


def sum_constraint_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def smooth_objective(x):
    n = int(G_N)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    scale = float(G_SCALE)
    if scale <= 0.0:
        scale = 1e-6

    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    vals = corr * (2.0 / float(n))
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    t = (vals - mx) / scale
    e = np.exp(t)
    return float(mx + scale * np.log(float(np.sum(e))))


def smooth_jacobian(x):
    n = int(G_N)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    scale = float(G_SCALE)
    if scale <= 0.0:
        scale = 1e-6

    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    vals = corr * (2.0 / float(n))
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    t = (vals - mx) / scale
    e = np.exp(t)
    w = e / float(np.sum(e))

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    active = np.where(w > 1e-10)[0]
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    for p in active:
        p = int(p)
        wp = float(w[p])
        k = p - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            grad[k:n] += (wp * dx) * (1.0 - x[:n - k])
            grad[:n - k] -= (wp * dx) * x[k:n]
        else:
            ell = -k
            grad[:n - ell] += (wp * dx) * (1.0 - x[ell:n])
            grad[ell:n] -= (wp * dx) * x[:n - ell]

    return grad


def slsqp_polish(h, n, budget_s):
    global G_N, G_SCALE, G_TARGET

    start = time.time()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget_s)) - 2.0

    h = make_feasible(h, n).copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    constraints = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": sum_constraint,
            "jac": sum_constraint_jac,
            "args": (n,)
        }
    ]

    scales = [1e-2, 5e-3, 2e-3, 1e-3, 5e-4, 2e-4]
    for scale in scales:
        if time.time() > deadline - 4.0:
            break

        G_N = n
        G_SCALE = float(scale)
        G_TARGET = 0.5 * float(n)

        maxiter = 220
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_objective,
                h,
                method="SLSQP",
                jac=smooth_jacobian,
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=constraints,
                options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-12}
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            hr = make_feasible(res.x, n)
            cr = compute_c5(hr)
            if cr < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = hr.copy()
                best_c = cr
                h = hr.copy()
            else:
                h = best_h.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def get_candidate_ns(nbase):
    max_n = max(int(nbase), 512)
    base_list = [
        int(nbase),
        2 * int(nbase),
        3 * int(nbase),
        4 * int(nbase),
        192, 256, 320, 384, 512
    ]
    ns = []
    for v in base_list:
        if v < 16:
            continue
        if v % 2 == 1:
            v += 1
        if v <= max_n and v not in ns:
            ns.append(v)
    return sorted(ns)


def make_start_list(n, base_h, base_n, rng, max_starts):
    starts = []
    if base_h is None:
        for v in range(3):
            starts.append(scored_binary(n, rng, v))
            if len(starts) >= max_starts:
                break
        return starts[:max_starts]

    if n == base_n:
        starts.append(base_h.copy())
        if max_starts > 1:
            starts.append(to_binary_feasible(base_h, n))
    else:
        hi = interpolate_to_n(base_h, n)
        starts.append(hi)
        if max_starts > 1:
            starts.append(to_binary_feasible(hi, n))

    variants = [0, 1, 2]
    for v in variants:
        if len(starts) >= max_starts:
            break
        starts.append(scored_binary(n, rng, v))

    return starts[:max_starts]


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    start_time = time.time()
    total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = min(10.0, 0.05 * total_budget)
    hard_deadline = start_time + total_budget - reserve

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        best_n = int(parent.size)
        if best_n % 2 == 1:
            parent = interpolate_to_n(parent, best_n + 1)
            best_n += 1
        best_h = make_feasible(parent, best_n)
    else:
        best_n = 160
        best_h = make_feasible(scored_binary(best_n, rng, 0), best_n)

    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    try:
        rem = hard_deadline - time.time()
        if rem > 80.0:
            pair_budget = min(500.0, max(30.0, 0.40 * rem))
            hopt, _ = pair_local_search(best_h, best_n, pair_budget, rng,
                                        full_pairs=(best_n <= 256))
            cnp = compute_c5(hopt)
            if cnp < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = hopt.copy()
                best_c = cnp
                best_n = len(best_h)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_n <= 256:
        rem = hard_deadline - time.time()
        if rem > 180.0:
            slbudget = min(120.0, 0.09 * total_budget, rem - 80.0)
            if slbudget > 30.0:
                try:
                    hsl, _ = slsqp_polish(best_h, best_n, slbudget)
                    csl = compute_c5(hsl)
                    if csl < best_c - 1e-14:
                        best_h = hsl.copy()
                        best_c = csl
                        best_n = len(best_h)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    candidate_ns = get_candidate_ns(best_n)
    other_ns = [n for n in candidate_ns if n != best_n]

    if other_ns:
        for n in other_ns:
            if time.time() > hard_deadline - 160.0:
                break
            rem = hard_deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 90.0:
                break

            max_starts = 2 if n > 400 else 3
            starts = make_start_list(n, best_h, best_n, rng, max_starts)
            if not starts:
                continue

            budget_total = min(180.0, max(30.0, rem - 30.0))
            budget_each = max(15.0, budget_total / float(len(starts)))

            for h0 in starts:
                if time.time() > hard_deadline - 160.0:
                    break
                b = min(budget_each, hard_deadline - time.time() - 160.0)
                if b < 12.0:
                    break
                try:
                    hopt, _ = pair_local_search(h0, n, b, rng,
                                                full_pairs=(n <= 256))
                    cnp = compute_c5(hopt)
                    if cnp < best_c - 1e-14:
                        best_h = hopt.copy()
                        best_c = cnp
                        best_n = len(best_h)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    rem = hard_deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 60.0:
        final_pair_budget = min(300.0, rem - 30.0)
        try:
            hopt, _ = pair_local_search(best_h, best_n, final_pair_budget, rng,
                                        full_pairs=(best_n <= 256))
            cnp = compute_c5(hopt)
            if cnp < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = hopt.copy()
                best_c = cnp
                best_n = len(best_h)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_n <= 256:
        rem = hard_deadline - time.time()
        if rem > 50.0:
            slbudget = min(60.0, rem - 15.0)
            if slbudget > 25.0:
                try:
                    hsl, _ = slsqp_polish(best_h, best_n, slbudget)
                    csl = compute_c5(hsl)
                    if csl < best_c - 1e-14:
                        best_h = hsl.copy()
                        best_c = csl
                        best_n = len(best_h)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    best_h = make_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    best_n = int(len(best_h))

    return best_h, float(best_c), best_n
```
